src/vision/line_buffer_3x3.v
来源：D:\yilingsi_fpga\Efinity_Project\技术文档\最新项目\10_13_ding_Ti60_Vision8_Gesture_ROI_Integrated\src\vision\line_buffer_3x3.v
原始文件SHA256：1d2ed996a21eb9b31ae408797c8510f768e025d5548924ebc40f4dfef65e48e7
========================================================================
//======================================================================
//  line_buffer_3x3.v
//----------------------------------------------------------------------
//  3x3 滑动窗口行缓存。Canny 链路（高斯 / 中值 / Sobel / NMS）的公共基础。
//
//  设计要点：
//    * 内部 2 块同步读 BRAM：prev_line_1（上一行）、prev_line_2（上两行）。
//      “当前行”不是 BRAM，而是把输入流延迟 1 拍后直接进移位寄存器，
//      这样和 BRAM 的 1 拍同步读延迟天然对齐，省掉 1 块 BRAM。
//    * BRAM 采用“读地址 = 当前列，写地址 = 当前列-1”的错位写法：
//      同一拍内读/写地址永远不同（避免同地址读写冲突），
//      并且读出的永远是“本帧内已经写完整的上/上上行”，不会读到当前行。
//    * 三条流（当前行 / 上一行 / 上两行）在输出拍全部对齐到同一列 k，
//      各自再经过 3 抽头移位寄存器，即得到以列 (k-1) 为中心的 3x3 窗口。
//
//  时序 / 坐标约定（重要，下游模块必须按此对齐）：
//      输入：de_i=1 时 x_i / y_i / data_i 有效（x_i 从 0 开始，y_i 从 0 开始）
//      输出：window_valid 有效那一拍，
//              x_o = 窗口中心列 = 输入列 - 1
//              y_o = 窗口中心行 = 输入行 - 1
//      窗口像素与 window_o 的位序：
//               window_o = {row2_sr, row1_sr, cur_sr}
//               row2_sr  = {p00, p01, p02}   // y_o-1 行，x_o-1 / x_o / x_o+1
//               row1_sr  = {p10, p11, p12}   // y_o   行
//               cur_sr   = {p20, p21, p22}   // y_o+1 行
//         即 p00 在最高位 [9*DW-1 : 8*DW]，p22 在最低位 [DW-1 : 0]。
//         每条行内 3 抽头寄存器均为 {最左(老), 中间, 最右(新)}。
//
//  有效窗口出现条件：
//      window_valid = de 延迟1拍 && (x_d1 >= 2) && (row_ready == 2)
//      x_d1 >= 2   -> 中心列 x_o >= 1，左右邻列都在本行内
//      row_ready==2 -> 本帧已经完整处理过 2 行，上/上上行数据真实有效
//      因此第 0、1 行不产生窗口，第 2 行从 x_o=1 开始产生窗口，
//      最后一行最后一列 x_o = WIDTH-2 仍然有效，最右侧 1 列（x_o=WIDTH-1）
//      因为右邻列越界而不输出（3x3 固有边界，属预期行为）。
//
//  frame_start：
//      每帧开始给 1 个（或以上）clk 的高电平脉冲，用于清 row_ready 计数器。
//      建议直接接 VSync 上升沿打一拍产生的单周期脉冲（de_i=0 期间给出）。
//      若该信号被长时间拉高，则 row_ready 一直为 0，模块不输出有效窗口。
//
//  WIDTH 支持 640 / 1280（AWIDTH 相应取 10 / 11）。
//======================================================================
`timescale 1ns/1ps

module line_buffer_3x3 #(
	parameter WIDTH  = 1280,	// 行宽（像素数）
	parameter DWIDTH = 8,		// 像素位宽
	parameter AWIDTH = 11		// 列地址位宽，需满足 2**AWIDTH >= WIDTH
)(
	input  wire					clk,
	input  wire					rst_n,			// 同步复位，低有效
	input  wire					frame_start,	// 帧起始脉冲（高有效）
	input  wire					de_i,			// 数据使能
	input  wire [AWIDTH-1:0]	x_i,			// 输入列坐标
	input  wire [9:0]			y_i,			// 输入行坐标
	input  wire [DWIDTH-1:0]	data_i,			// 输入像素

	output wire [9*DWIDTH-1:0]	window_o,		// 3x3 窗口
	output reg					window_valid,	// 窗口有效（与 x_o/y_o 同拍）
	output reg  [AWIDTH-1:0]	x_o,			// 窗口中心列
	output reg  [9:0]			y_o				// 窗口中心行
);

	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	// 0. 输入流打 1 拍
	//    cur_d1  : 当前行像素（与 BRAM 同步读输出对齐）
	//    x_d1    : 列坐标延迟 1 拍（同时当作 BRAM 写地址）
	//    y_d1    : 行坐标延迟 1 拍
	//    de_d1   : 数据使能延迟 1 拍（写使能 + 移位使能）
	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	reg [DWIDTH-1:0]	cur_d1;
	reg [AWIDTH-1:0]	x_d1;
	reg [9:0]			y_d1;
	reg					de_d1;

	wire				dv = de_i && (x_i < WIDTH);	// 地址合法保护

	always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
		if (!rst_n) begin
			cur_d1 <= {DWIDTH{1'b0}};
			x_d1   <= {AWIDTH{1'b0}};
			y_d1   <= 10'd0;
			de_d1  <= 1'b0;
		end else begin
			if (de_i) begin
				cur_d1 <= data_i;
				x_d1   <= (x_i < WIDTH) ? x_i : {AWIDTH{1'b0}};
				y_d1   <= y_i;
			end
			de_d1 <= dv;
		end
	end

	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	// 1. 两块同步读 BRAM：prev_line_1 / prev_line_2
	//    读地址 = x_i（当拍列），输出延迟 1 拍得到“上一行同列”像素。
	//    写地址 = x_d1（前当拍列），与读地址错开 1，读写不同址。
	//    prev_line_2 的写入数据取 prev_line_1 的读出数据，形成 2 级行延迟链。
	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	(* ram_style = "block" *) (* ramstyle = "block" *)
	reg [DWIDTH-1:0] prev_line_1 [0:WIDTH-1];
	(* ram_style = "block" *) (* ramstyle = "block" *)
	reg [DWIDTH-1:0] prev_line_2 [0:WIDTH-1];

	reg [DWIDTH-1:0] rd1_dout;
	reg [DWIDTH-1:0] rd2_dout;

	always @(posedge clk) begin
		if (de_d1) begin
			prev_line_1[x_d1] <= cur_d1;		// 写入当前行
			prev_line_2[x_d1] <= rd1_dout;		// 级联写入上一行
		end
		rd1_dout <= prev_line_1[x_i];			// 同步读 -> 上一行同列
		rd2_dout <= prev_line_2[x_i];			// 同步读 -> 上两行同列
	end

	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	// 2. 每条行的 3 抽头移位寄存器
	//    sr 位序 = {最左, 中间, 最右}，最右为最新进来的像素。
	//    行首（x_d1==0）先清零，保证不把上一行行尾串进来。
	//    移位只在 de_d1==1 时进行，因此消隐期不会拉入无效数据。
	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	reg [3*DWIDTH-1:0] cur_sr;	// y   行：(x-2, x-1, x)
	reg [3*DWIDTH-1:0] row1_sr;	// y-1 行
	reg [3*DWIDTH-1:0] row2_sr;	// y-2 行

	// Cascaded cropped streams start at x=1,2,3..., not x=0.
	reg previous_valid;
	reg [9:0] previous_y;
	reg [1:0] samples_in_row;
	wire new_line = de_d1 && (!previous_valid || y_d1 != previous_y);
	always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
		if (!rst_n) begin previous_valid<=0; previous_y<=0; samples_in_row<=0; end
		else if(frame_start) begin previous_valid<=0; samples_in_row<=0; end
		else begin
			previous_valid<=de_d1;
			if(de_d1) begin
				previous_y<=y_d1;
				if(new_line) samples_in_row<=1;
				else if(samples_in_row<2) samples_in_row<=samples_in_row+1'b1;
			end
		end
	end

	always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
		if (!rst_n) begin
			cur_sr  <= {3*DWIDTH{1'b0}};
			row1_sr <= {3*DWIDTH{1'b0}};
			row2_sr <= {3*DWIDTH{1'b0}};
		end else if (de_d1) begin
			if (new_line) begin
				// 行首：填入唯一一个有效样本，其余置 0（本拍窗口必无效）
				cur_sr  <= {{2*DWIDTH{1'b0}}, cur_d1};
				row1_sr <= {{2*DWIDTH{1'b0}}, rd1_dout};
				row2_sr <= {{2*DWIDTH{1'b0}}, rd2_dout};
			end else begin
				// 最新样本进低位，原内容向高位推：丢弃最高 DWIDTH 位（最老的抽头）
				cur_sr  <= {cur_sr[2*DWIDTH-1:0], cur_d1};
				row1_sr <= {row1_sr[2*DWIDTH-1:0], rd1_dout};
				row2_sr <= {row2_sr[2*DWIDTH-1:0], rd2_dout};
			end
		end
	end

	// 窗口输出：移位后一拍，sr 恰好覆盖 (x_o-1, x_o, x_o+1)
	assign window_o = {row2_sr, row1_sr, cur_sr};

	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	// 3. 行就绪计数：每行结束 +1，饱和到 2
	//    只有 row_ready==2 时才说明 prev_line_1/2 里是本帧的真实行。
	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	reg		de_q;
	wire	de_fall = de_q & ~de_i;		// 行结束（DE 下降沿）

	reg [1:0] row_ready;

	always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
		if (!rst_n) begin
			de_q      <= 1'b0;
			row_ready <= 2'd0;
		end else begin
			de_q <= de_i;
			if (frame_start) begin
				row_ready <= 2'd0;
			end else if (de_fall && (row_ready != 2'd2)) begin
				row_ready <= row_ready + 2'd1;
			end
		end
	end

	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	// 4. 输出坐标与有效标志（与上面移位后的 sr 同拍）
	//    sr 移位发生在 de_d1 那一拍，输出在下一拍：
	//      x_o = x_d1 - 1,  y_o = y_d1 - 1
	//    组合逻辑产生，保证与 window_o（组合取自 sr）严格同拍。
	//------------------------------------------------------------------
	reg we_next;
	reg [AWIDTH-1:0] x_o_d;
	reg [9:0]        y_o_d;

	always @(*) begin
		we_next = de_d1 && (samples_in_row == 2) && (row_ready == 2'd2) && !new_line && !frame_start;
		x_o_d   = x_d1 - {{(AWIDTH-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1};
		y_o_d   = y_d1 - 10'd1;
	end

	always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
		if (!rst_n) begin
			window_valid <= 1'b0;
			x_o          <= {AWIDTH{1'b0}};
			y_o          <= 10'd0;
		end else begin
			window_valid <= we_next;
			x_o          <= x_o_d;
			y_o          <= y_o_d;
		end
	end

endmodule

//  by CrazyBird
module vision8_ddr #(
	parameter 	C_ID_LEN      = 8, 
			C_DATA_LEN    = 128, 
			C_DATA_SIZE   = 4, 	//	C_DATA_LEN=8:0, 16:1, 32:2, 64:3, 128:4, 256:5, 512:6, 1024:7
			
			C_BURST_LEN   = 128,
			
			C_STRB_LEN    = C_DATA_LEN / 8, 
			C_ADDR_INC    = C_BURST_LEN * C_STRB_LEN,
			
			C_BASE_ADDR   = 32'h00000000, 	//	Start at 0x00000000 by default
			C_BUF_SIZE    = 22, 			//	Allocate 2^22 bytes (4MB) by default. The module takes 4*4MB by default. 
			C_RD_END_ADDR = 1280 * 720,
			
			FRAME_W = 1280, FRAME_H = 720,
            C_W_WIDTH     = 64, 
			C_R_WIDTH     = 8
)(
    input  wire                 axi_clk         ,
    input  wire                 axi_reset       ,
    
    output       [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_awid        ,
    output       [31:0]      axi_awaddr      ,
    output       [ 7:0]      axi_awlen       ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_awsize      ,
    output       [ 1:0]      axi_awburst     ,
    output                   axi_awlock      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_awcache     ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_awprot      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_awqos       ,
    output wire                  axi_awvalid     ,
    input  wire                 axi_awready     ,
    
    output       [C_DATA_LEN-1:0]      axi_wdata       ,
    output       [C_STRB_LEN-1:0]      axi_wstrb       ,
    output                   axi_wlast       ,
    output wire                  axi_wvalid      ,
    input  wire                 axi_wready      ,
    
    input  wire     [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_bid         ,
    input  wire     [ 1:0]      axi_bresp       ,
    input  wire                 axi_bvalid      ,
    output                   axi_bready      ,
    
    output       [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_arid        ,
    output       [31:0]      axi_araddr      ,
    output       [ 7:0]      axi_arlen       ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_arsize      ,
    output       [ 1:0]      axi_arburst     ,
    output                   axi_arlock      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_arcache     ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_arprot      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_arqos       ,
    output wire                  axi_arvalid     ,
    input  wire                 axi_arready     ,
    
    input  wire     [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_rid         ,
    input  wire     [C_DATA_LEN-1:0]      axi_rdata       ,
    input  wire     [ 1:0]      axi_rresp       ,
    input  wire                 axi_rlast       ,
    input  wire                 axi_rvalid      ,
    output                   axi_rready      ,
    
    input  wire                 wframe_pclk     ,
    input  wire                 wframe_vsync    ,		//	Writter VSync. Flush on falling edge. Connect to EOF. 
    input  wire                 wframe_data_en  ,
    input  wire     [C_W_WIDTH-1:0]      wframe_data     ,
    
    input  wire                 rframe_pclk     ,
    input  wire                 rframe_vsync    ,		//	Reader VSync. Flush on falling edge. Connect to ~EOF. 
    input  wire                 rframe_data_en  ,
    output wire     [C_R_WIDTH-1:0]      rframe_data     ,
    
    output 		[31:0] 	tp_o,
    input wire freeze_request,
    output wire frame_ready,
    output wire freeze_active,
 input main_enable,input aux_request,input[1:0]aux_kind,input[15:0]aux_directory,
 output reg aux_ack,aux_frame_ready,
 input snap_begin,snap_end,input[2:0]snap_slot,input snap_bad,snap_reset,snap_wr,
 input[31:0]snap_data,output snap_full,
 input aux_rd,output[31:0]aux_data,output aux_empty,
 output reg snap_done,snap_ok,output[31:0]source_id,
 output[31:0]base_errors,output reg[31:0]memory_errors,output reg main_frame_ok
);
 wire[31:0]c_awaddr,c_araddr;wire[7:0]c_awlen,c_arlen;
 wire c_awvalid,c_awready,c_arvalid,c_arready,c_wvalid,c_wready,c_wlast,c_bready;
 wire[127:0]c_wdata;wire[1:0]raw_slot;
 wire[31:0]unused_tp;
 reg[1:0]write_owner,read_owner;reg write_turn,read_turn;
 reg[31:0]waddr,raddr;reg[7:0]wlen,rlen;
 reg awvalid,arvalid,write_data,write_response;
 reg aux_protect;reg[1:0]aux_slot;
 wire[31:0]snap_q;wire snap_empty;wire[10:0]snap_wrlevel,snap_level;
 reg[127:0]packed_data;reg[1:0]pack_index;reg packed_valid;
 reg[5:0]snap_beat;reg[19:0]snapshot_bytes;reg snapshot_running,snapshot_failed;
 reg[2:0]snapshot_slot;reg[26:0]snapshot_watchdog;
 (* async_reg="true" *)reg sbm,sbs,sem,ses,badm,bads;
 reg sb_seen,se_seen;
 wire aux_full;wire[10:0]aux_level,aux_rdlevel;
 reg aux_reset,aux_push;reg[31:0]aux_wdata;
 (* async_reg="true" *)reg arm,ars;
 reg[1:0]kind;reg[15:0]dir;
 reg[9:0]line;reg half_line;reg[11:0]offset;reg[7:0]aux_beats;reg[7:0]return_beats;
 reg[6:0]reset_cycles;reg restart,drop_returns,aux_failed;
 reg[127:0]split_data;reg[2:0]split_remaining;reg[1:0]split_index;
 wire camera_read=read_owner==1,camera_write=write_owner==1;
 wire snap_pop=snapshot_running&&write_owner==2&&write_data&&!packed_valid&&!snap_empty;
 wire snapshot_request=snapshot_running&&snapshot_bytes<FRAME_W*FRAME_H/2&&snap_level>=128;
 wire[31:0]snapshot_address=32'h01000000+{10'd0,snapshot_slot,19'd0}+snapshot_bytes;
 wire[1:0]tile={line>=FRAME_H/2,half_line};
 wire tile_valid=dir[12+tile];
 wire[2:0]physical=dir[tile*3+:3];
 wire end_aux=kind==1?line>=FRAME_H/4:line>=FRAME_H;
 wire auxiliary_request=kind!=0&&!end_aux&&!restart&&!aux_reset&&aux_level<768&&split_remaining==0&&(kind==1||tile_valid);

 vision8_base_axi #(.C_ID_LEN(C_ID_LEN),.C_DATA_LEN(C_DATA_LEN),.C_DATA_SIZE(C_DATA_SIZE),
 .C_BURST_LEN(C_BURST_LEN),.C_BASE_ADDR(C_BASE_ADDR),.C_BUF_SIZE(C_BUF_SIZE),
 .C_RD_END_ADDR(C_RD_END_ADDR),.C_W_WIDTH(C_W_WIDTH),.C_R_WIDTH(C_R_WIDTH)) camera(
 .axi_clk(axi_clk),.axi_reset(axi_reset),
 .axi_awid(),.axi_awaddr(c_awaddr),.axi_awlen(c_awlen),.axi_awsize(),.axi_awburst(),.axi_awlock(),.axi_awcache(),.axi_awprot(),.axi_awqos(),
 .axi_awvalid(c_awvalid),.axi_awready(c_awready),.axi_wdata(c_wdata),.axi_wstrb(),.axi_wlast(c_wlast),.axi_wvalid(c_wvalid),.axi_wready(c_wready),
 .axi_bid(axi_bid),.axi_bresp(axi_bresp),.axi_bvalid(axi_bvalid&&camera_write&&write_response),.axi_bready(c_bready),
 .axi_arid(),.axi_araddr(c_araddr),.axi_arlen(c_arlen),.axi_arsize(),.axi_arburst(),.axi_arlock(),.axi_arcache(),.axi_arprot(),.axi_arqos(),
 .axi_arvalid(c_arvalid),.axi_arready(c_arready),.axi_rid(axi_rid),.axi_rdata(axi_rdata),.axi_rresp(axi_rresp),
 .axi_rlast(axi_rlast),.axi_rvalid(axi_rvalid&&camera_read),.axi_rready(),
 .wframe_pclk(wframe_pclk),.wframe_vsync(wframe_vsync),.wframe_data_en(wframe_data_en),.wframe_data(wframe_data),
 .rframe_pclk(rframe_pclk),.rframe_vsync(rframe_vsync),.rframe_data_en(rframe_data_en),.rframe_data(rframe_data),
 .tp_o(unused_tp),.freeze_request(freeze_request),.frame_ready(frame_ready),.freeze_active(freeze_active),
 .main_enable(main_enable),.raw_slot(raw_slot),.source_id(source_id),.axi_errors(base_errors),.aux_protect(aux_protect),.aux_slot(aux_slot));
 assign axi_awid=0;assign axi_arid=0;
 assign axi_awsize=C_DATA_SIZE;assign axi_arsize=C_DATA_SIZE;
 assign axi_awburst=1;assign axi_arburst=1;assign axi_awlock=0;assign axi_arlock=0;
 assign axi_awcache=0;assign axi_arcache=0;assign axi_awprot=0;assign axi_arprot=0;assign axi_awqos=0;assign axi_arqos=0;
 assign axi_awaddr=waddr;assign axi_araddr=raddr;assign axi_awlen=wlen;assign axi_arlen=rlen;
 assign axi_awvalid=awvalid;assign axi_arvalid=arvalid;
 assign c_awready=awvalid&&axi_awready&&camera_write;
 assign c_arready=arvalid&&axi_arready&&camera_read;
 assign c_wready=axi_wready&&camera_write&&write_data;
 assign axi_wstrb={C_STRB_LEN{1'b1}};
 assign axi_wdata=camera_write?c_wdata:packed_data;
 assign axi_wvalid=write_data&&(camera_write?c_wvalid:packed_valid);
 assign axi_wlast=camera_write?c_wlast:snap_beat==31;
 assign axi_bready=write_response&&(camera_write?c_bready:1'b1);

 vision8_fifo snapshot_fifo(rframe_pclk,axi_clk,axi_reset||snap_reset,snap_wr,snap_data,snap_pop,snap_q,snap_full,snap_empty,snap_wrlevel,snap_level);

 vision8_fifo auxiliary_fifo(axi_clk,rframe_pclk,axi_reset||aux_reset,aux_push,aux_wdata,aux_rd,aux_data,aux_full,aux_empty,aux_level,aux_rdlevel);
 wire[31:0]auxiliary_address=kind==1?
  {8'd0,aux_slot,22'd0}+line*(FRAME_W*8)+offset:
  32'h01000000+{10'd0,physical,19'd0}+(line>=FRAME_H/2?line-FRAME_H/2:line)*FRAME_W+offset;
 wire[12:0]aux_remaining=(kind==1?FRAME_W*2:FRAME_W)-offset;
 wire[12:0]boundary_space=4096-{1'b0,auxiliary_address[11:0]};
 wire[12:0]candidate_bytes=aux_remaining<512?aux_remaining:13'd512;
 wire[12:0]aux_transfer=candidate_bytes<boundary_space?candidate_bytes:boundary_space;
 assign axi_rready=camera_read?1'b1:(split_remaining==0&&!aux_full);
 assign tp_o={memory_errors[15:0],aux_level[7:0],snapshot_running,aux_failed,restart,read_owner,write_owner,packed_valid};
 always @(posedge axi_clk or posedge axi_reset)begin
  if(axi_reset)begin
   write_owner<=0;read_owner<=0;write_turn<=0;read_turn<=0;waddr<=0;raddr<=0;wlen<=0;rlen<=0;awvalid<=0;arvalid<=0;write_data<=0;write_response<=0;
   aux_protect<=0;aux_slot<=0;snap_done<=0;snap_ok<=0;memory_errors<=0;main_frame_ok<=0;packed_data<=0;pack_index<=0;packed_valid<=0;snap_beat<=0;snapshot_bytes<=0;
   snapshot_running<=0;snapshot_failed<=0;snapshot_slot<=0;snapshot_watchdog<=0;sbm<=0;sbs<=0;sem<=0;ses<=0;badm<=0;bads<=0;sb_seen<=0;se_seen<=0;
   aux_reset<=1;aux_push<=0;aux_wdata<=0;aux_ack<=0;aux_frame_ready<=0;arm<=0;ars<=0;kind<=0;dir<=0;line<=0;half_line<=0;offset<=0;aux_beats<=0;return_beats<=0;
   reset_cycles<=0;restart<=1;drop_returns<=1;aux_failed<=0;split_data<=0;split_remaining<=0;split_index<=0;
  end else begin
   sbm<=snap_begin;sbs<=sbm;sem<=snap_end;ses<=sem;badm<=snap_bad;bads<=badm;arm<=aux_request;ars<=arm;
   aux_push<=0;
   if(sbs!=sb_seen&&!snapshot_running&&write_owner==0)begin
    sb_seen<=sbs;snapshot_running<=1;snapshot_bytes<=0;snapshot_slot<=snap_slot;snapshot_failed<=0;packed_valid<=0;pack_index<=0;se_seen<=ses;snapshot_watchdog<=0;
   end
   if(snapshot_running)begin
    if(snapshot_watchdog!=27'h7ffffff)snapshot_watchdog<=snapshot_watchdog+1'b1;
    if(ses!=se_seen&&(snapshot_bytes==FRAME_W*FRAME_H/2||snapshot_watchdog==27'h7ffffff)&&write_owner==0)begin
     snap_ok<=snapshot_bytes==FRAME_W*FRAME_H/2&&!snapshot_failed&&!bads;snap_done<=~snap_done;snapshot_running<=0;se_seen<=ses;
    end
   end
   if(write_owner==0)begin
    if(c_awvalid&&(!snapshot_request||!write_turn))begin write_owner<=1;waddr<=c_awaddr;wlen<=c_awlen;awvalid<=1;write_turn<=1;end
    else if(snapshot_request)begin write_owner<=2;waddr<=snapshot_address;wlen<=31;awvalid<=1;write_turn<=0;snap_beat<=0;packed_valid<=0;pack_index<=0;end
   end
   if(awvalid&&axi_awready)begin awvalid<=0;write_data<=1;end
   if(snap_pop)begin packed_data[pack_index*32+:32]<=snap_q;if(pack_index==3)begin pack_index<=0;packed_valid<=1;end else pack_index<=pack_index+1'b1;end
   if(axi_wvalid&&axi_wready)begin
    if(write_owner==2)begin packed_valid<=0;snap_beat<=snap_beat+1'b1;end
    if(axi_wlast)begin write_data<=0;write_response<=1;end
   end
   if(axi_bvalid&&axi_bready)begin
    if(write_owner==2)begin snapshot_bytes<=snapshot_bytes+512;if(axi_bresp!=0||axi_bid!=0)snapshot_failed<=1;end
    if(axi_bresp!=0||axi_bid!=0)memory_errors<=memory_errors+1'b1;
    write_response<=0;write_owner<=0;
   end
   // A new raster command cancels only queued work. Accepted returns still drain.
   if(ars!=aux_ack)begin restart<=1;drop_returns<=1;aux_frame_ready<=0;end
   if(restart&&read_owner==0&&split_remaining==0)begin
    aux_reset<=1;reset_cycles<=0;kind<=aux_kind;dir<=aux_directory;aux_slot<=raw_slot;aux_protect<=aux_kind==1;
    line<=0;half_line<=0;offset<=0;aux_failed<=0;restart<=0;aux_ack<=ars;
   end else if(aux_reset&&!restart)begin
    if(reset_cycles==63)begin aux_reset<=0;drop_returns<=0;end else reset_cycles<=reset_cycles+1'b1;
   end
   if(kind!=0&&!aux_reset&&!restart&&!aux_failed&&aux_level>=((FRAME_W/2)<640?FRAME_W/2:640))aux_frame_ready<=1;
   if(kind==2&&!tile_valid&&!restart&&!aux_reset&&read_owner==0)begin
    if(half_line)begin half_line<=0;line<=line+1'b1;end else half_line<=1;
   end
   if(read_owner==0&&!restart&&!aux_reset)begin
    if(c_arvalid&&(!auxiliary_request||!read_turn))begin read_owner<=1;raddr<=c_araddr;rlen<=c_arlen;arvalid<=1;read_turn<=1;return_beats<=0;end
    else if(auxiliary_request)begin
     read_owner<=2;raddr<=auxiliary_address;rlen<=aux_transfer/16-1;arvalid<=1;read_turn<=0;aux_beats<=31;return_beats<=0;
    end
   end else if(read_owner==0&&(restart||aux_reset)&&c_arvalid)begin
    // MAIN drain/prefill continues while the AUX FIFO is reset.
    read_owner<=1;raddr<=c_araddr;rlen<=c_arlen;arvalid<=1;return_beats<=0;
   end
   if(arvalid&&axi_arready)begin arvalid<=0;if(camera_read&&raddr[C_BUF_SIZE-1:0]==0)main_frame_ok<=1;end
   if(axi_rvalid&&axi_rready)begin
    return_beats<=return_beats+1'b1;
    if(axi_rresp!=0||axi_rid!=0||axi_rlast!=(return_beats==rlen))begin
     memory_errors<=memory_errors+1'b1;
     if(read_owner==2)begin aux_failed<=1;aux_frame_ready<=0;end
     else main_frame_ok<=0;
    end
    if(read_owner==2&&!drop_returns&&!restart&&axi_rresp==0&&axi_rid==0)begin
     if(kind==1)begin aux_push<=1;aux_wdata<={axi_rdata[63:48],axi_rdata[127:112]};end
     else begin split_data<=axi_rdata;split_remaining<=4;split_index<=0;end
    end
    if(axi_rlast)begin
     read_owner<=0;
     if(!camera_read)begin
      if(offset+((rlen+1)*16)>=(kind==1?FRAME_W*2:FRAME_W))begin
       offset<=0;
       if(kind==1)line<=line+1'b1;
       else if(half_line)begin half_line<=0;line<=line+1'b1;end else half_line<=1;
      end else offset<=offset+((rlen+1)*16);
     end
    end
   end
   if(split_remaining!=0&&!aux_full)begin
    if(!drop_returns&&!restart)begin aux_push<=1;aux_wdata<=split_data[split_index*32+:32];end
    split_remaining<=split_remaining-1'b1;split_index<=split_index+1'b1;
   end
  end
 end
endmodule

//  by CrazyBird
module vision8_base_axi #(
	parameter 	C_ID_LEN      = 8, 
			C_DATA_LEN    = 128, 
			C_DATA_SIZE   = 4, 	//	C_DATA_LEN=8:0, 16:1, 32:2, 64:3, 128:4, 256:5, 512:6, 1024:7
			
			C_BURST_LEN   = 128,
			
			C_STRB_LEN    = C_DATA_LEN / 8, 
			C_ADDR_INC    = C_BURST_LEN * C_STRB_LEN,
			
			C_BASE_ADDR   = 32'h00000000, 	//	Start at 0x00000000 by default
			C_BUF_SIZE    = 22, 			//	Allocate 2^22 bytes (4MB) by default. The module takes 4*4MB by default. 
			C_RD_END_ADDR = 1280 * 720,
			
			C_W_WIDTH     = 64, 
			C_R_WIDTH     = 8
)(
    input  wire                 axi_clk         ,
    input  wire                 axi_reset       ,
    
    output       [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_awid        ,
    output       [31:0]      axi_awaddr      ,
    output       [ 7:0]      axi_awlen       ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_awsize      ,
    output       [ 1:0]      axi_awburst     ,
    output                   axi_awlock      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_awcache     ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_awprot      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_awqos       ,
    output reg                  axi_awvalid     ,
    input  wire                 axi_awready     ,
    
    output       [C_DATA_LEN-1:0]      axi_wdata       ,
    output       [C_STRB_LEN-1:0]      axi_wstrb       ,
    output                   axi_wlast       ,
    output reg                  axi_wvalid      ,
    input  wire                 axi_wready      ,
    
    input  wire     [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_bid         ,
    input  wire     [ 1:0]      axi_bresp       ,
    input  wire                 axi_bvalid      ,
    output                   axi_bready      ,
    
    output       [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_arid        ,
    output       [31:0]      axi_araddr      ,
    output       [ 7:0]      axi_arlen       ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_arsize      ,
    output       [ 1:0]      axi_arburst     ,
    output                   axi_arlock      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_arcache     ,
    output       [ 2:0]      axi_arprot      ,
    output       [ 3:0]      axi_arqos       ,
    output reg                  axi_arvalid     ,
    input  wire                 axi_arready     ,
    
    input  wire     [C_ID_LEN-1:0]      axi_rid         ,
    input  wire     [C_DATA_LEN-1:0]      axi_rdata       ,
    input  wire     [ 1:0]      axi_rresp       ,
    input  wire                 axi_rlast       ,
    input  wire                 axi_rvalid      ,
    output                   axi_rready      ,
    
    input  wire                 wframe_pclk     ,
    input  wire                 wframe_vsync    ,		//	Writter VSync. Flush on falling edge. Connect to EOF. 
    input  wire                 wframe_data_en  ,
    input  wire     [C_W_WIDTH-1:0]      wframe_data     ,
    
    input  wire                 rframe_pclk     ,
    input  wire                 rframe_vsync    ,		//	Reader VSync. Flush on falling edge. Connect to ~EOF. 
    input  wire                 rframe_data_en  ,
    output wire     [C_R_WIDTH-1:0]      rframe_data     ,
    
    output 		[31:0] 	tp_o,
    input wire freeze_request,
    output wire frame_ready,
    output wire freeze_active,
 input main_enable,
 output wire[1:0] raw_slot,output wire[31:0]source_id,output reg[31:0]axi_errors,
 input aux_protect,input[1:0]aux_slot
); 
	reg 				r_w_rst = 1;
 wire camera_full,camera_fifo_overflow,camera_reset_busy;reg camera_overflow;

	reg 	[3:0] 	rs_w = 0; 
 reg [21:0] camera_bytes;
 reg camera_vs_q,camera_frame_good;
 (* async_reg="true" *)reg frame_good_meta,frame_good_sync;
 wire [1:0] rc_wframe_index,rc_rframe_index;
 reg[31:0]slot_id[0:3];reg[31:0]sequence_id;reg writer_failed;
 integer si;
 reg r_wframe_inc=0,r_rframe_inc=0;
	wire 				w_wfifo_pempty, w_wfifo_empty; 
	wire 				w_wfifo_ren; 
	wire 	[C_DATA_LEN-1:0] 	w_wfifo_rdata; 
	reg 	[C_BUF_SIZE-1:0] 	rc_w_ptr = 0; 
	reg 	[0:0] 		rc_w_eof = 0; 
	reg 	[1:0] 	r_wframe_sync = 0; 	
	reg 			r_weof_pending = 0; 
reg                             rframe_vsync_dly;
wire                            rframe_vsync_neg;
reg             [4:0]           rfifo_cnt;
reg                             rfifo_rst;
reg                             rfifo_wenb;
reg             [C_DATA_LEN-1:0]          rfifo_wdata;
wire            [ 9:0]          rfifo_wcnt;
wire 					  rfifo_wfull; 
wire                            rfifo_wr_rst_busy;
wire 					w_rfifo_aempty; 
wire 					w_rfifo_empty; 
 (* async_reg="true" *)reg reset_meta,reset_sync,enable_meta,enable_sync;
 reg reset_pending,reset_previous;reg[5:0]settle;
 reg[1:0]rd_state;reg[C_BUF_SIZE-1:0]araddr;
 reg[7:0]read_beats;
	wire 	[3:0] 	ws_w_idle = 0; 
	wire 	[3:0] 	ws_w_wdata = 1; 
	wire 	[3:0] 	ws_w_winc = 2; 
	wire 	[3:0] 	ws_w_eof = 3; 
wire 			w_rfifo_rst = axi_reset || rfifo_rst; 


	
	initial begin
		axi_awvalid <= 0; 
		axi_wvalid <= 0; 
		axi_arvalid <= 0; 
	end

	assign axi_awid    = {C_ID_LEN{1'b0}};
	assign axi_awlen   = C_BURST_LEN - 1'b1;
	assign axi_awsize  = C_DATA_SIZE;
	assign axi_awburst = 2'b01;	//	INCR
	assign axi_awlock  = 1'b0;
	assign axi_awcache = 0;
	assign axi_awprot  = 3'b0;
	assign axi_awqos   = 4'b0;
	assign axi_wstrb   = 32'hFFFFFFFF;
	assign axi_bready = (rs_w==ws_w_winc); 
	    
	assign axi_arid    = {C_ID_LEN{1'b0}};
	assign axi_arlen   = C_BURST_LEN - 1'b1;
	assign axi_arsize  = C_DATA_SIZE;
	assign axi_arburst = 2'b01;	//	INCR
	assign axi_arlock  = 1'b0;
	assign axi_arcache = 0;
	assign axi_arprot  = 3'b0;
	assign axi_arqos   = 4'b0;
	assign axi_rready = 1; 
	
	
	
	
	////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
	// Count camera bytes independently of AXI burst padding. A short frame
 // may occupy 900 padded bursts but is still not a complete camera frame.
 always @(posedge wframe_pclk or posedge axi_reset)
 if(axi_reset)begin camera_bytes<=0;camera_vs_q<=0;camera_frame_good<=0;camera_overflow<=0;end
 else begin
   camera_vs_q<=wframe_vsync;
   if(camera_vs_q&&!wframe_vsync)begin
     camera_frame_good<=(camera_bytes==C_RD_END_ADDR)&&!camera_overflow;camera_bytes<=0;camera_overflow<=0;
   end else if(wframe_data_en)begin
     if(camera_bytes<C_RD_END_ADDR+4096)camera_bytes<=camera_bytes+C_W_WIDTH/8;
     if(camera_full||camera_fifo_overflow||r_w_rst||camera_reset_busy)camera_overflow<=1;
   end
 end
 always @(posedge axi_clk or posedge axi_reset)
 if(axi_reset)begin frame_good_meta<=0;frame_good_sync<=0;end
 else begin frame_good_meta<=camera_frame_good;frame_good_sync<=frame_good_meta;end

 //	R/W Schedule
 assign raw_slot=rc_rframe_index;assign source_id=slot_id[rc_rframe_index];
 always @(posedge axi_clk or posedge axi_reset)
 if(axi_reset)begin sequence_id<=0;for(si=0;si<4;si=si+1)slot_id[si]<=0;end
 else if(r_wframe_inc)begin sequence_id<=sequence_id+1'b1;slot_id[rc_wframe_index]<=sequence_id+1'b1;end
 always @(posedge axi_clk or posedge axi_reset)
 if(axi_reset)begin writer_failed<=0;axi_errors<=0;end
 else begin
  if(rs_w==ws_w_eof)writer_failed<=0;
  if(axi_bvalid&&axi_bready&&(axi_bresp!=0||axi_bid!=0))begin writer_failed<=1;axi_errors<=axi_errors+1'b1;end
  if(axi_rvalid&&axi_rready&&(axi_rresp!=0||axi_rid!=0))axi_errors<=axi_errors+1'b1;
 end
 vision8_slots u_frame_slots(
   .clk(axi_clk),.reset(axi_reset),.write_done(r_wframe_inc),.read_start(r_rframe_inc),
   .freeze_async(freeze_request),.write_slot(rc_wframe_index),.read_slot(rc_rframe_index),
   .frozen(freeze_active),.have_frame(frame_ready),.aux_protect(aux_protect),.aux_slot(aux_slot));
	
	
	
	////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
	//	AXI Writter


	//	On EOF when ~empty, flush the last data then reset FIFO. 

	//	Write Pointer. Burst bytes is always C_ADDR_INC. 
	
	//	Burst Control
	reg 	[7:0] 	rc_burst = 0; 	//	Compare to C_BURST_LEN - 1. 
	
	//	EOF Monitor. 

	always @(posedge axi_clk or posedge axi_reset) begin
		if(axi_reset) begin
			rs_w <= 0; 
			rc_w_ptr <= 0; 
			rc_w_eof <= 0; 
			r_w_rst <= 1; 
			axi_awvalid <= 0; 
			axi_wvalid <= 0; 
			r_wframe_sync <= 0; 
			r_weof_pending <= 0; 
			r_wframe_inc <= 0; 
			
		end else begin
			rc_w_eof <= 0; 
			r_wframe_inc <= 0; 
			
			//	Raise EOF on falling edge of VSYNC. 
			r_wframe_sync <= {r_wframe_sync, wframe_vsync}; 
			if(r_wframe_sync == 2'b10) begin
				r_weof_pending <= 1; 
			end else begin
			end
			
			if(axi_awready) begin
				axi_awvalid <= 0; 
			end else begin
			end
			
			case (rs_w)
				ws_w_idle: begin
						rc_burst <= 0; 
						r_w_rst <= 0; 
						
						if(~w_wfifo_pempty) begin
							axi_awvalid <= 1; 
							axi_wvalid <= 1; 
							rs_w <= ws_w_wdata; 
						
						end else if(r_weof_pending && (~w_wfifo_empty)) begin
							//	There's some data in the FIFO. write last. 
							axi_awvalid <= 1; 
							axi_wvalid <= 1; 
							rs_w <= ws_w_wdata; 
							
						end else if(r_weof_pending) begin
							//	EOF. Reset FIFO. Increment write pointer. 
							r_w_rst <= 1; 
							r_wframe_inc <= (rc_w_ptr == C_RD_END_ADDR) && frame_good_sync && !writer_failed; 
							rs_w <= ws_w_eof; 
							
						end else begin
						end
					end
					
				ws_w_eof: begin
						//	Wait for some cycles to release reset. 
						r_weof_pending <= 0; 
						rc_w_ptr <= 0; 
						
						rc_w_eof <= rc_w_eof + 1; 
						if(&rc_w_eof)
							rs_w <= ws_w_idle; 
						else begin
						end
					end
					
				ws_w_wdata: begin
						rc_burst <= rc_burst + axi_wready; 
						
						//	On last transaction terminate write. 
						if(axi_wlast && axi_wready) begin
							axi_wvalid <= 0; 
							rs_w <= ws_w_winc; 
						end else begin
						end
					end
					
				ws_w_winc: begin
                         if(axi_bvalid && axi_bready && !axi_awvalid)begin
                             rc_w_ptr <= rc_w_ptr + C_ADDR_INC;
                             rs_w <= ws_w_idle;
                         end
                         end
			endcase
		end
	end
	assign axi_awaddr = C_BASE_ADDR + {rc_wframe_index, rc_w_ptr}; 
	assign axi_wdata = w_wfifo_rdata; 
	assign axi_wlast = (rc_burst >= C_BURST_LEN - 1); 
	
	assign w_wfifo_ren = axi_wvalid && axi_wready; 
	
	
	assign tp_o[1:0] = {rs_w[1:0]}; 
	assign tp_o[2] = axi_wvalid; 
	assign tp_o[3] = wframe_data_en; 
	
	
	generate
		if(C_W_WIDTH == 64) begin: Gen_FIFO_W64
			W0_FIFO_64 u_W0_FIFO_64 
			(
			    .full_o(camera_full),.overflow_o(camera_fifo_overflow),.rst_busy(camera_reset_busy),
                .wr_clk_i       (wframe_pclk    ),
			    .wr_en_i        (wframe_data_en),
			    .wdata          (wframe_data    ),
			    
			    .a_rst_i 	(r_w_rst), 

			    .rd_clk_i       (axi_clk        ),
			    .rd_en_i        (w_wfifo_ren     ),
			    .rdata          (w_wfifo_rdata    ),
			    .prog_empty_o	(w_wfifo_pempty),
			    .empty_o 		(w_wfifo_empty)
			);
		end else if(C_W_WIDTH == 32) begin: Gen_FIFO_W32
			W0_FIFO_32 u_W0_FIFO_32
			(
			    .full_o(camera_full),.overflow_o(camera_fifo_overflow),.rst_busy(camera_reset_busy),
                .wr_clk_i       (wframe_pclk    ),
			    .wr_en_i        (wframe_data_en),
			    .wdata          (wframe_data    ),
			    
			    .a_rst_i 	(r_w_rst), 

			    .rd_clk_i       (axi_clk        ),
			    .rd_en_i        (w_wfifo_ren     ),
			    .rdata          (w_wfifo_rdata    ),
			    .prog_empty_o	(w_wfifo_pempty),
			    .empty_o 		(w_wfifo_empty)
			);
		end else if(C_W_WIDTH == 16) begin: Gen_FIFO_W32
			W0_FIFO_16 u_W0_FIFO_16
			(
			    .full_o(camera_full),.overflow_o(camera_fifo_overflow),.rst_busy(camera_reset_busy),
                .wr_clk_i       (wframe_pclk    ),
			    .wr_en_i        (wframe_data_en),
			    .wdata          (wframe_data    ),
			    
			    .a_rst_i 	(r_w_rst), 

			    .rd_clk_i       (axi_clk        ),
			    .rd_en_i        (w_wfifo_ren     ),
			    .rdata          (w_wfifo_rdata    ),
			    .prog_empty_o	(w_wfifo_pempty),
			    .empty_o 		(w_wfifo_empty)
			);
		end else if(C_W_WIDTH == 8) begin: Gen_FIFO_W8
			W0_FIFO_8 u_W0_FIFO_8 
			(
			    .full_o(camera_full),.overflow_o(camera_fifo_overflow),.rst_busy(camera_reset_busy),
                .wr_clk_i       (wframe_pclk    ),
			    .wr_en_i        (wframe_data_en),
			    .wdata          (wframe_data    ),
			    
			    .a_rst_i 	(r_w_rst), 

			    .rd_clk_i       (axi_clk        ),
			    .rd_en_i        (w_wfifo_ren     ),
			    .rdata          (w_wfifo_rdata    ),
			    .prog_empty_o	(w_wfifo_pempty),
			    .empty_o 		(w_wfifo_empty)
			);
		end
	endgenerate





















always @(posedge rframe_pclk)
begin
    if(axi_reset == 1'b1)
        rframe_vsync_dly <= 1'b0;
    else
        rframe_vsync_dly <= rframe_vsync;
end

assign rframe_vsync_neg =  rframe_vsync_dly & ~rframe_vsync;


always @(posedge rframe_pclk)
begin
    if(axi_reset == 1'b1)
        rfifo_cnt <= 5'h1f;
    else
    begin
        if(rframe_vsync_neg == 1'b1)
            rfifo_cnt <= 5'h1f;
        else if(rfifo_cnt > 5'd0)
            rfifo_cnt <= rfifo_cnt - 1'b1;
        else
            rfifo_cnt <= 5'b0;
    end
end


always @(posedge rframe_pclk)
begin
    if(axi_reset == 1'b1)
        rfifo_rst <= 1'b1;
    else
    begin
        if(rfifo_cnt == 5'b0)
            rfifo_rst <= 1'b0;
        else
            rfifo_rst <= 1'b1;
    end
end





generate
	if(C_R_WIDTH == 8) begin: Gen_RFIFO_8
		R0_FIFO_8 u_R0_FIFO_8
		(
			.wr_clk_i		(axi_clk),
			.wr_en_i		(rfifo_wenb),
			.wdata			(rfifo_wdata),
			.a_rst_i		(w_rfifo_rst), 
			.prog_full_o		(rfifo_wfull),
			
			.rd_clk_i		(rframe_pclk),
			.rd_en_i		(rframe_data_en),
			.rdata			(rframe_data),
			.empty_o		(w_rfifo_empty),
			.almost_empty_o	(w_rfifo_aempty)
		);
	end else if(C_R_WIDTH == 16) begin: Gen_RFIFO_16	
		R0_FIFO_16 u_R0_FIFO_16
		(
			.wr_clk_i		(axi_clk),
			.wr_en_i		(rfifo_wenb),
			.wdata			(rfifo_wdata),
			.a_rst_i		(w_rfifo_rst), 
			.prog_full_o		(rfifo_wfull),
			
			.rd_clk_i		(rframe_pclk),
			.rd_en_i		(rframe_data_en),
			.rdata			(rframe_data),
			.empty_o		(w_rfifo_empty),
			.almost_empty_o	(w_rfifo_aempty)
		);
	end
endgenerate
		
// Keep descriptors and the slot protected until all returns drain.
 always @(posedge axi_clk or posedge axi_reset)begin
  if(axi_reset)begin reset_meta<=1;reset_sync<=1;reset_previous<=1;enable_meta<=0;enable_sync<=0;reset_pending<=1;settle<=0;
   rd_state<=0;araddr<=0;axi_arvalid<=0;rfifo_wenb<=0;rfifo_wdata<=0;r_rframe_inc<=0;read_beats<=0;end
  else begin
   reset_meta<=w_rfifo_rst;reset_sync<=reset_meta;reset_previous<=reset_sync;enable_meta<=main_enable;enable_sync<=enable_meta;
   rfifo_wenb<=0;r_rframe_inc<=0;
   if(reset_sync&&!reset_previous)reset_pending<=1;
   if(rd_state==0)begin
    if(reset_pending||reset_sync)begin
     araddr<=0;settle<=0;
     if(!reset_sync)begin reset_pending<=0;r_rframe_inc<=1;end
    end else if(settle!=63)settle<=settle+1'b1;
    else if(enable_sync&&frame_ready&&araddr<C_RD_END_ADDR&&!rfifo_wfull)begin axi_arvalid<=1;rd_state<=1;read_beats<=0;end
   end else if(rd_state==1)begin
    if(axi_arvalid&&axi_arready)begin axi_arvalid<=0;rd_state<=2;end
   end else if(axi_rvalid)begin
    read_beats<=read_beats+1'b1;
    rfifo_wdata<=axi_rdata;
    rfifo_wenb<=!reset_pending&&!reset_sync&&axi_rresp==0&&axi_rid==0;
    if(axi_rlast)begin araddr<=araddr+C_ADDR_INC;rd_state<=0;end
   end
  end
 end
 assign axi_araddr=C_BASE_ADDR+{rc_rframe_index,araddr};
endmodule

// Pixel-domain capture directory, pre-HUD downsampling and final-raster compositor.
// Five DDR slots permit replacing a full four-tile directory atomically.
module vision8_frames #(parameter WIDTH=1280,HEIGHT=720)(
 input clk,rst_n,input[2:0]mode_i,input[1:0]algorithm_i,
 input pip_i,capture_i,clear_i,freeze_active,input[31:0]source_id,
 input[11:0]low_i,high_i,input[15:0]version_i,input source_ok,processing_ok,
 input[3:0]face_count,input face_valid,face_saturated,
 input[23:0]rgb_i,input de_i,vs_i,hs_i,
 input[15:0]result_i,input result_de,result_vs,input[10:0]result_x,input[9:0]result_y,
 output reg[23:0]rgb_o,output reg de_o,vs_o,hs_o,
 output busy,lock_source,main_enable,output[2:0]mode_committed,output[11:0]capture_low,capture_high,output[3:0]capture_backend,
 output reg snap_begin,snap_end,output reg[2:0]snap_slot,output reg snap_bad,snap_reset,
 output reg snap_wr,output reg[31:0]snap_data,input snap_full,
 input snap_done,snap_ok,
 output reg aux_request,output reg[1:0]aux_kind,output reg[15:0]aux_directory,
 input aux_ack,input[31:0]aux_data,input aux_empty,input aux_frame_ready,
 output aux_rd,output reg[3:0]valid_tiles,
 output reg[511:0]metadata,output reg[31:0]underflows,capture_errors);
 localparam HALF_W=WIDTH/2,HALF_H=HEIGHT/2,PIXELS=HALF_W*HALF_H;
 reg[2:0]state,mode_active;reg[1:0]warm,next_tile;reg[2:0]temporary;
 reg vsq,rvsq,pair_phase;reg[15:0]first_pixel;
 reg[18:0]samples;reg cancelled;reg[6:0]reset_count;
 reg[1:0]bound_alg;reg[11:0]bound_low,bound_high;reg[15:0]bound_version;reg[31:0]bound_id;
 reg[11:0]directory,pending_directory;reg[3:0]pending_valid;reg publish;
 reg[2:0]old_slot;reg[511:0]pending_metadata;
 reg done_meta,done_sync,done_seen,ok_meta,ok_sync;
 reg ack_meta,ack_sync,ready_meta,ready_sync;
 reg[10:0]x;reg[9:0]y;reg pixel_half,invalid_frame,pip_active;
 wire frame_blank=vs_i&&!vsq,process_start=result_vs&&!rvsq;
 wire[1:0]tile={y>=HALF_H,x>=HALF_W};
 wire pip_area=x>=WIDTH-WIDTH/4-16&&x<WIDTH-16&&y>=16&&y<16+HEIGHT/4;
 wire compare_pixel=mode_active==4&&valid_tiles[tile];
 wire use_aux=de_i&&(compare_pixel||(pip_active&&pip_area));
 wire read_pixel=use_aux&&!invalid_frame&&ready_sync&&!aux_empty;
 assign aux_rd=read_pixel&&pixel_half;
 wire[15:0]aux_pixel=pixel_half?aux_data[31:16]:aux_data[15:0];
 wire[23:0]expanded={aux_pixel[15:11],aux_pixel[15:13],aux_pixel[10:5],aux_pixel[10:9],aux_pixel[4:0],aux_pixel[4:2]};
 wire[10:0]local_x=x>=HALF_W?x-HALF_W:x;
 wire[9:0]local_y=y>=HALF_H?y-HALF_H:y;
 wire[127:0]tile_meta=metadata[tile*128+:128];
 reg[383:0]label;reg[7:0]label_char;reg[10:0]label_x;reg[9:0]label_y;
 reg label_area;integer c;reg[11:0]label_low,label_high;
 function[7:0]hexchar;input[3:0]n;begin hexchar=n<10?8'h30+n:8'h41+n-10;end endfunction
 function[7:0]digit;input[11:0]v;input[1:0]pos;
 begin case(pos)0:digit=48+v/1000;1:digit=48+(v/100)%10;2:digit=48+(v/10)%10;default:digit=48+v%10;endcase end endfunction
 always @*begin
  label=0;label_char=32;label_x=0;label_y=0;label_area=0;c=0;label_low=low_i;label_high=high_i;
  if(mode_active==4)begin
   label_x=local_x-8;label_y=local_y-8;label_area=local_x>=8&&local_x<584&&local_y>=8&&local_y<24;
   if(valid_tiles[tile])begin
    case(tile_meta[97:96])0:label={"RAW   L:0000 H:0000 F:00000000 V:0000",88'd0};1:label={"CANNY L:0000 H:0000 F:00000000 V:0000",88'd0};
     2:label={"RED   L:0040 H:0040 F:00000000 V:0000",88'd0};default:label={"CYBER L:0040 H:0040 F:00000000 V:0000",88'd0};endcase
    label_low=tile_meta[83:72];label_high=tile_meta[95:84];
   end else label={"EMPTY - IO29 CAPTURE",232'd0};
  end else if(mode_active>=5)begin
   label_x=x-16;label_y=y-8;label_area=x>=16&&x<592&&y>=8&&y<40;
   case(mode_active)
    5:label={"06 THRESH L:0000 H:0000",200'd0};
    6:label={"07 FACE CANDIDATES: - ",216'd0};
    7:label={"08 GESTURE - BOARD K1 ENROL",176'd0};
   endcase
  end
  c=label_x/12;
  if(c<48)label_char=label[383-c*8-:8];
  if(mode_active==4&&valid_tiles[tile])begin
   if(c>=8&&c<12&&tile_meta[97:96]<2)label_char=digit(label_low,c-8);
   if(c>=15&&c<19&&tile_meta[97:96]<2)label_char=digit(label_high,c-15);
   if(c>=22&&c<30)label_char=hexchar(tile_meta[24+(29-c)*4+:4]);
   if(c>=33&&c<37)label_char=hexchar(tile_meta[56+(36-c)*4+:4]);
  end
  if(mode_active==5)begin
   if(c>=12&&c<16)label_char=digit(label_low,c-12);
   if(c>=19&&c<23)label_char=digit(label_high,c-19);
  end
  if(mode_active==6&&c==19)label_char=face_valid?hexchar(face_count):"-";
  if(mode_active==6&&c==20)label_char=face_saturated?"+":" ";
 end
 wire label_ink;
 vision8_ascii text(label_char,mode_active==4?label_y[3:1]:label_y[4:2],(label_x%12)>>1,label_ink);
 assign mode_committed=mode_active;
 assign busy=state!=0||publish;
 assign lock_source=state!=0;
 assign main_enable=mode_i!=4||state!=0;
 assign capture_low=bound_low;assign capture_high=bound_high;
 assign capture_backend=bound_alg==0?4'd0:bound_alg==1?4'd1:bound_alg==2?4'd10:4'd9;
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  if(!rst_n)begin
   state<=0;mode_active<=0;warm<=0;next_tile<=0;temporary<=4;vsq<=1;rvsq<=1;pair_phase<=0;first_pixel<=0;samples<=0;cancelled<=0;reset_count<=0;
   bound_alg<=0;bound_low<=40;bound_high<=80;bound_version<=0;bound_id<=0;directory<=12'h688;pending_directory<=12'h688;pending_valid<=0;publish<=0;old_slot<=0;pending_metadata<=0;
   snap_begin<=0;snap_end<=0;snap_slot<=4;snap_bad<=0;snap_reset<=1;snap_wr<=0;snap_data<=0;aux_request<=0;aux_kind<=0;aux_directory<=0;
   valid_tiles<=0;metadata<=0;underflows<=0;capture_errors<=0;done_meta<=0;done_sync<=0;done_seen<=0;ok_meta<=0;ok_sync<=0;
   ack_meta<=0;ack_sync<=0;ready_meta<=0;ready_sync<=0;x<=0;y<=0;pixel_half<=0;invalid_frame<=0;pip_active<=0;rgb_o<=0;de_o<=0;vs_o<=0;hs_o<=0;
  end else begin
   vsq<=vs_i;rvsq<=result_vs;snap_wr<=0;
   done_meta<=snap_done;done_sync<=done_meta;ok_meta<=snap_ok;ok_sync<=ok_meta;
   ack_meta<=aux_ack;ack_sync<=ack_meta;ready_meta<=aux_frame_ready;ready_sync<=ready_meta;
   if(!vs_i)begin x<=0;y<=0;end else if(de_i)begin if(x==WIDTH-1)begin x<=0;y<=y+1'b1;end else x<=x+1'b1;end
   de_o<=de_i;vs_o<=vs_i;hs_o<=hs_i;rgb_o<=de_i?rgb_i:24'd0;
   if(mode_active==4&&de_i)rgb_o<=24'h142235;
   if(read_pixel)begin rgb_o<=expanded;pixel_half<=~pixel_half;end
   else if(use_aux&&!invalid_frame&&(!ready_sync||aux_empty))begin invalid_frame<=1;underflows<=underflows+1'b1;end
   if(pip_active&&de_i&&x>=WIDTH-WIDTH/4-18&&x<WIDTH-14&&y>=14&&y<18+HEIGHT/4&&
    (x<WIDTH-WIDTH/4-16||x>=WIDTH-16||y<16||y>=16+HEIGHT/4))rgb_o<=24'h35d499;
   if(mode_active==4&&de_i&&(x==HALF_W||y==HALF_H))rgb_o<=24'h35d499;
   if(label_area&&de_i)rgb_o<=label_ink?24'he7f3ff:24'h132336;
   if(frame_blank)begin
    mode_active<=mode_i;pip_active<=pip_i&&mode_i>=1&&mode_i<=3;pixel_half<=0;invalid_frame<=0;
    if(publish)begin directory<=pending_directory;valid_tiles<=pending_valid;metadata<=pending_metadata;publish<=0;end
    if(clear_i)begin valid_tiles<=0;pending_valid<=0;next_tile<=0;publish<=0;metadata<=0;end
    if(ack_sync==aux_request)begin
     aux_kind<=mode_i==4?2'd2:(pip_i&&mode_i>=1&&mode_i<=3?2'd1:2'd0);
     aux_directory<=publish?{pending_valid,pending_directory}:{valid_tiles,directory};
     aux_request<=~aux_request;
    end else invalid_frame<=1;
   end
   if(clear_i)begin cancelled<=1;valid_tiles<=0;pending_valid<=0;publish<=0;next_tile<=0;metadata<=0;end
   if(state!=0&&mode_i!=4)cancelled<=1;
   case(state)
    0:begin snap_reset<=1;if(capture_i&&mode_i==4&&source_ok&&ack_sync==aux_request)begin state<=1;warm<=0;cancelled<=0;bound_alg<=algorithm_i;bound_low<=low_i;bound_high<=high_i;bound_version<=version_i;end end
    1:if(process_start&&freeze_active)begin warm<=0;bound_id<=source_id;state<=2;end
    2:if(process_start)begin
     if(source_id!=bound_id)begin bound_id<=source_id;warm<=0;end
     else if(warm==1)begin state<=3;reset_count<=0;snap_slot<=temporary;end else warm<=warm+1'b1;
    end
    3:begin
     snap_reset<=0;
     if(reset_count==63)begin snap_begin<=~snap_begin;state<=4;end else reset_count<=reset_count+1'b1;
    end
    4:if(process_start)begin state<=5;samples<=0;pair_phase<=0;snap_bad<=cancelled||!source_ok||!processing_ok;end
    5:begin
     if(result_de&&!result_x[0]&&!result_y[0])begin
      samples<=samples+1'b1;
      if(!pair_phase)begin first_pixel<=result_i;pair_phase<=1;end
      else begin snap_wr<=!snap_full;snap_data<={result_i,first_pixel};pair_phase<=0;if(snap_full)snap_bad<=1;end
      if(result_x==WIDTH-2&&result_y==HEIGHT-2)begin
       snap_bad<=snap_bad||snap_full||cancelled||clear_i||!source_ok||!processing_ok||source_id!=bound_id||samples!=PIXELS-1;
       snap_end<=~snap_end;state<=6;
      end
     end
    end
    6:if(done_sync!=done_seen)begin
     done_seen<=done_sync;
     if(ok_sync&&!snap_bad&&!cancelled&&!clear_i&&mode_i==4)begin
      old_slot=directory[next_tile*3+:3];
      pending_directory<=directory;pending_directory[next_tile*3+:3]<=temporary;
      pending_valid<=valid_tiles|(4'b1<<next_tile);pending_metadata<=metadata;
      pending_metadata[next_tile*128+:128]<={30'd0,bound_alg,bound_high,bound_low,bound_version,bound_id,24'd0};
      temporary<=old_slot;next_tile<=next_tile+1'b1;publish<=1;
     end else capture_errors<=capture_errors+1'b1;
     state<=0;
    end
   endcase
  end
 end
endmodule

module vision8_slots(
 input clk,reset,write_done,read_start,freeze_async,
 input aux_protect,input[1:0]aux_slot,
 output reg[1:0]write_slot,read_slot,output reg frozen,have_frame);
 (* async_reg="true" *)reg fm,fs;
 reg[1:0]latest;reg displayed;integer i;reg found;reg[1:0]candidate,next_write;
 always @*begin
  found=0;next_write=write_slot;
  for(i=1;i<4;i=i+1)begin
   candidate=write_slot+i;
   if(!found&&candidate!=read_slot&&(!aux_protect||candidate!=aux_slot))begin next_write=candidate;found=1;end
  end
 end
 always @(posedge clk or posedge reset)begin
  if(reset)begin fm<=0;fs<=0;write_slot<=0;read_slot<=2;latest<=0;displayed<=0;frozen<=0;have_frame<=0;end
  else begin
   fm<=freeze_async;fs<=fm;
   if(write_done)begin write_slot<=next_write;latest<=write_slot;have_frame<=1;end
   if(read_start)begin
    if(fs&&have_frame&&displayed)frozen<=1;
    else begin frozen<=0;if(write_done)read_slot<=write_slot;else if(have_frame)read_slot<=latest;end
    if(write_done||have_frame)displayed<=1;
   end
  end
 end
endmodule

// Dual-clock 32-bit FIFO, registered RAM prefetch; no combinational RAM read.
// Pointer-only reset must be requested after outstanding DDR traffic drains.
module vision8_fifo #(parameter AB=10)(
 input wr_clk,rd_clk,reset,input wr_en,input[31:0]wr_data,
 input rd_en,output reg[31:0]rd_data,output full,empty,
 output[AB:0]wr_level,rd_level);
 (* ram_style="block" *)reg[31:0]mem[0:(1<<AB)-1];
 reg[AB:0]wp,rp,wg,rg;
 (* async_reg="true" *)reg[AB:0]rm,rs,wm,ws;
 function[AB:0]decode;input[AB:0]g;integer i;begin decode[AB]=g[AB];for(i=AB-1;i>=0;i=i-1)decode[i]=decode[i+1]^g[i];end endfunction
 wire[AB:0]wn=wp+(wr_en&&!full),rn=rp+(rd_en&&!empty);
 assign wr_level=wp-decode(rs);assign rd_level=decode(ws)-rp;
 assign full=wr_level==(1<<AB);assign empty=(rg==ws);
 always @(posedge wr_clk or posedge reset)begin
  if(reset)begin wp<=0;wg<=0;rm<=0;rs<=0;end
  else begin rm<=rg;rs<=rm;wp<=wn;wg<=wn^(wn>>1);if(wr_en&&!full)mem[wp[AB-1:0]]<=wr_data;end
 end
 always @(posedge rd_clk)rd_data<=mem[rn[AB-1:0]];
 always @(posedge rd_clk or posedge reset)begin
  if(reset)begin rp<=0;rg<=0;wm<=0;ws<=0;end
  else begin wm<=wg;ws<=wm;rp<=rn;rg<=rn^(rn>>1);end
 end
endmodule

// Preserve the existing IO28 normalizer. IO29 and board keys are active low.
// Context is bound at the debounced press; changing mode cancels held actions.
module vision8_key #(parameter DEBOUNCE=1488000,LONG_CYCLES=148800000)(
 input clk,rst_n,key_n,input[2:0]context_i,
 output reg short_o,long_o);
 localparam DB=DEBOUNCE<2?1:$clog2(DEBOUNCE),LB=LONG_CYCLES<2?1:$clog2(LONG_CYCLES);
 (* async_reg="true" *)reg meta,sync;
 reg stable,armed,held,fired,cancel;reg[2:0]bound;
 reg[DB-1:0]deb;reg[LB-1:0]duration;
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  if(!rst_n)begin meta<=0;sync<=0;stable<=0;armed<=0;held<=0;fired<=0;cancel<=0;bound<=0;deb<=0;duration<=0;short_o<=0;long_o<=0;end
  else begin
   meta<=key_n;sync<=meta;short_o<=0;long_o<=0;
   if(held&&context_i!=bound)cancel<=1;
   if(sync==stable)deb<=0;
   else if(deb==DEBOUNCE-1)begin
    deb<=0;stable<=sync;
    if(sync)begin armed<=1;if(held&&!fired&&!cancel&&context_i==bound)short_o<=1;held<=0;duration<=0;end
    else if(armed)begin armed<=0;held<=1;fired<=0;cancel<=0;bound<=context_i;duration<=0;end
   end else deb<=deb+1'b1;
   if(held&&!stable&&!fired&&!cancel&&context_i==bound)begin
    if(duration==LONG_CYCLES-1)begin fired<=1;long_o<=1;end else duration<=duration+1'b1;
   end
  end
 end
endmodule

module vision8_controls #(parameter DEBOUNCE=1488000,LONG_CYCLES=148800000)(
 input clk,rst_n,frame_start,io28,io29,k1_n,k2_n,
 input capture_busy,learning,rx,
 output reg[2:0]user_mode,output reg[1:0]capture_alg,
 output reg pip_enable,freeze_request,output reg capture,clear,
 output reg gesture_short,gesture_long,
 output reg[11:0]low,high,output reg[1:0]selected,step_select,
 output reg[11:0]low_active,high_active,output reg[15:0]version,
 output[3:0]backend_mode,output reg pure_o,detect_extra,output reg[7:0]skin_lower);
 wire[3:0]s,l;
 vision8_key #(.DEBOUNCE(DEBOUNCE),.LONG_CYCLES(LONG_CYCLES)) a(clk,rst_n,~io28,user_mode,s[0],l[0]);
 vision8_key #(.DEBOUNCE(DEBOUNCE),.LONG_CYCLES(LONG_CYCLES)) b(clk,rst_n,io29,user_mode,s[1],l[1]);
 vision8_key #(.DEBOUNCE(DEBOUNCE),.LONG_CYCLES(LONG_CYCLES)) c(clk,rst_n,k1_n,user_mode,s[2],l[2]);
 vision8_key #(.DEBOUNCE(DEBOUNCE),.LONG_CYCLES(LONG_CYCLES)) d(clk,rst_n,k2_n,user_mode,s[3],l[3]);
 wire[11:0]step=step_select==0?12'd1:step_select==1?12'd4:12'd8;
 wire[11:0]inc_limit=selected==0?high-1:12'd2040;
 wire[11:0]dec_limit=selected==1?low+1:12'd1;
 reg[11:0]delta;
 wire serial_valid;wire[7:0]serial_data;reg diagnostic;reg[3:0]diagnostic_mode;
 vision8_uart serial(clk,rst_n,rx,serial_valid,serial_data);
 wire[1:0]algorithm=user_mode==4?capture_alg:user_mode[1:0];
 assign backend_mode=diagnostic?diagnostic_mode:user_mode==7?4'd15:user_mode==5?4'd1:user_mode==6?4'd0:
  algorithm==0?4'd0:algorithm==1?4'd1:algorithm==2?4'd10:4'd9;
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  if(!rst_n)begin user_mode<=0;capture_alg<=0;pip_enable<=0;freeze_request<=0;capture<=0;clear<=0;gesture_short<=0;gesture_long<=0;
   low<=40;high<=80;low_active<=40;high_active<=80;selected<=0;step_select<=0;version<=0;delta<=0;pure_o<=0;detect_extra<=0;skin_lower<=10;diagnostic<=0;diagnostic_mode<=0;end
  else begin
   capture<=0;clear<=0;gesture_short<=0;gesture_long<=0;
   if(frame_start)begin
    low_active<=low;high_active<=high;
    if(low!=low_active||high!=high_active)version<=version+1'b1;
   end
   if(s[0])begin user_mode<=user_mode+1'b1;freeze_request<=0;diagnostic<=0;pure_o<=0;end
   else begin
    if(l[0]&&user_mode>=1&&user_mode<=3)pip_enable<=~pip_enable;
    if(user_mode==4)begin
     if(s[1]&&!capture_busy)capture<=1;
     if(l[1])clear<=1;
     if(s[3]&&!capture_busy)capture_alg<=capture_alg+1'b1;
    end else if(user_mode==5)begin
     if(s[2])selected<=selected==2?0:selected+1'b1;
     if(l[2])step_select<=step_select==2?0:step_select+1'b1;
     if(l[1])freeze_request<=~freeze_request;
     if(s[3])begin
      if(selected==2)begin delta=(2040-high<step)?2040-high:step;low<=low+delta;high<=high+delta;end
      else if(selected==0)low<=inc_limit-low<step?inc_limit:low+step;
      else high<=inc_limit-high<step?inc_limit:high+step;
     end else if(s[1])begin
      if(selected==2)begin delta=(low-1<step)?low-1:step;low<=low-delta;high<=high-delta;end
      else if(selected==1)high<=high-dec_limit<step?dec_limit:high-step;
      else low<=low-dec_limit<step?dec_limit:low-step;
     end
    end else if(user_mode==7)begin
     gesture_short<=s[2];gesture_long<=l[2];
     if(l[2])freeze_request<=0;
     else if(s[1]&&!learning)freeze_request<=~freeze_request;
    end else if(s[1])freeze_request<=~freeze_request;
   end
   // UART remains available for maintenance. Human modes are 0..7 here.
   if(serial_valid)begin
    if(serial_data>="0"&&serial_data<="7")begin user_mode<=serial_data-"0";diagnostic<=0;pure_o<=0;freeze_request<=0;end
    else case(serial_data)
     "m":begin user_mode<=user_mode+1'b1;diagnostic<=0;pure_o<=0;freeze_request<=0;end
     "+":begin delta=(2040-high<8)?2040-high:8;low<=low+delta;high<=high+delta;end
     "-":begin delta=(low-1<8)?low-1:8;low<=low-delta;high<=high-delta;end
     "r":begin user_mode<=0;low<=40;high<=80;selected<=0;step_select<=0;pure_o<=0;diagnostic<=0;detect_extra<=0;skin_lower<=10;freeze_request<=0;end
     "p":begin pure_o<=~pure_o;user_mode<=0;diagnostic<=0;end
     "f":detect_extra<=~detect_extra;
     "g":begin user_mode<=0;diagnostic<=1;diagnostic_mode<=5;pure_o<=0;end
     "c":begin user_mode<=0;diagnostic<=1;diagnostic_mode<=12;pure_o<=0;end
     "t":begin user_mode<=0;diagnostic<=1;diagnostic_mode<=8;pure_o<=0;end
     "[":if(skin_lower>5)skin_lower<=skin_lower-5;
     "]":if(skin_lower<85)skin_lower<=skin_lower+5;
    endcase
   end
  end
 end
endmodule

// Bundled-data toggle handshake: source payload stays fixed until acknowledged.
module menu_status_cdc #(parameter BITS=13) (
 input src_clk, src_reset, input [BITS-1:0] src_status,
 input dst_clk, dst_reset, output reg [BITS-1:0] dst_status
);
 reg [BITS-1:0] payload;
 reg request, acknowledge;
 (* async_reg="true" *) reg ack_meta, ack_sync;
 (* async_reg="true" *) reg req_meta, req_sync;
 always @(posedge src_clk or posedge src_reset)
 if(src_reset) begin payload<={BITS{1'b0}};request<=0;ack_meta<=0;ack_sync<=0;end
 else begin
   ack_meta<=acknowledge;ack_sync<=ack_meta;
   if(ack_sync==request && payload!=src_status) begin
     payload<=src_status;request<=~request;
   end
 end
 always @(posedge dst_clk or posedge dst_reset)
 if(dst_reset) begin req_meta<=0;req_sync<=0;acknowledge<=0;dst_status<={BITS{1'b0}};end
 else begin
   req_meta<=request;req_sync<=req_meta;
   if(req_sync!=acknowledge) begin dst_status<=payload;acknowledge<=req_sync;end
 end
endmodule

// Same two-cycle mask latency as ep1_skin; only mode 6 uses this module.
// Palm colour is sampled from a 32x32 crosshair. No camera registers are changed.
module gesture_skin_learn #(parameter WIDTH=1280,HEIGHT=720)(
 input clk,rst_n,de,fs,input[10:0]x,input[9:0]y,input[23:0]rgb,
 input[7:0]lower,input sample,clear,
 output reg mask,valid,frame_start,output reg[10:0]xo,output reg[9:0]yo,
 output reg calibrated,sample_done,sample_ok);
 localparam CX=WIDTH*7/16,CY=HEIGHT/2;
 reg[15:0]luma;reg[17:0]cb,cr;reg[7:0]diff,lo;reg red_blue,d,f;reg[10:0]xx;reg[9:0]yy;
 reg[7:0]cb_lo,cb_hi,cr_lo,cr_hi;
 reg pending,collect;reg[18:0]cb_sum,cr_sum,y_sum;reg[10:0]count;
 wire[7:0]cb_avg=cb_sum[17:10],cr_avg=cr_sum[17:10],y_avg=y_sum[17:10];
 wire colour_ok=count==1024&&cr_avg>=130&&cr_avg<=185&&cb_avg>=80&&cb_avg<=140&&y_avg>=40&&y_avg<=242;
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  if(!rst_n)begin
   cb<=0;cr<=0;luma<=0;diff<=0;lo<=10;red_blue<=0;d<=0;f<=0;xx<=0;yy<=0;
   mask<=0;valid<=0;frame_start<=0;xo<=0;yo<=0;
   calibrated<=0;sample_done<=0;sample_ok<=0;pending<=0;collect<=0;count<=0;cb_sum<=0;cr_sum<=0;y_sum<=0;
   cb_lo<=80;cb_hi<=140;cr_lo<=132;cr_hi<=185;
  end else begin
   luma<=rgb[23:16]*16'd77+rgb[15:8]*16'd150+rgb[7:0]*16'd29;
   cb<=18'd32768-rgb[23:16]*18'd43-rgb[15:8]*18'd85+rgb[7:0]*18'd128;
   cr<=18'd32768+rgb[23:16]*18'd128-rgb[15:8]*18'd107-rgb[7:0]*18'd21;
   diff<=rgb[23:16]>rgb[15:8]?rgb[23:16]-rgb[15:8]:8'd0;
   red_blue<=rgb[23:16]>rgb[7:0];lo<=lower;d<=de;f<=fs;xx<=x;yy<=y;
   mask<=d&&luma[15:8]>=28&&luma[15:8]<=250&&
     cb>={cb_lo,8'd0}&&cb<={cb_hi,8'd0}&&cr>={cr_lo,8'd0}&&cr<={cr_hi,8'd0}&&
     (calibrated||(diff>=(lo>5?lo-5:0)&&diff<100&&red_blue));
   valid<=d;frame_start<=f;xo<=xx;yo<=yy;
   sample_done<=0;
   if(clear)begin calibrated<=0;pending<=0;collect<=0;cb_lo<=80;cb_hi<=140;cr_lo<=132;cr_hi<=185;end
   else begin
    if(sample)pending<=1;
    if(f)begin
     if(collect)begin
      sample_done<=1;sample_ok<=colour_ok;
      if(colour_ok)begin
       // Luma-independent chroma window tolerates bright and shaded fingers.
       cb_lo<=cb_avg>24?cb_avg-24:0;cb_hi<=cb_avg<231?cb_avg+24:255;
       // Pale fingertips approach neutral chroma as brightness increases.
       cr_lo<=cr_avg<158?130:(cr_avg<163?cr_avg-28:135);
       cr_hi<=cr_avg<233?cr_avg+22:255;calibrated<=1;
      end
     end
     collect<=pending||sample;pending<=0;count<=0;cb_sum<=0;cr_sum<=0;y_sum<=0;
    end else if(collect&&d&&xx>=CX-16&&xx<CX+16&&yy>=CY-16&&yy<CY+16)begin
     cb_sum<=cb_sum+cb[15:8];cr_sum<=cr_sum+cr[15:8];y_sum<=y_sum+luma[15:8];count<=count+1'b1;
    end
   end
  end
 end
endmodule

// A normalized 16x16 silhouette from the previous complete candidate box.
// Only 256 sample bits are stored, not an image. CCL association validates it.
module gesture_sample16(
 input clk,rst_n,enable,fs,de,skin,input[10:0]x,input[9:0]y,
 input box_valid,input[41:0]box,
 output reg valid,output reg[41:0]sample_box,output reg[255:0]bitmap);
 reg active,pending;reg[41:0]ref_box;reg[255:0]working;reg[8:0]samples;
 reg[3:0]col,row;
 wire[10:0]bw=ref_box[21:11]-ref_box[10:0]+1;
 wire[9:0]bh=ref_box[41:32]-ref_box[31:22]+1;
 wire[16:0]xp=({1'b0,col,1'b1})*bw;
 wire[15:0]yp=({1'b0,row,1'b1})*bh;
 wire[10:0]tx=ref_box[10:0]+(xp>>5);
 wire[9:0]ty=ref_box[31:22]+(yp>>5);
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  if(!rst_n||!enable)begin active<=0;pending<=0;ref_box<=0;working<=0;samples<=0;col<=0;row<=0;valid<=0;sample_box<=0;bitmap<=0;end
  else if(fs)begin
   valid<=active&&samples==256;sample_box<=ref_box;bitmap<=working;
   active<=0;pending<=1;working<=0;samples<=0;col<=0;row<=0;
  end else if(pending)begin active<=box_valid;ref_box<=box;pending<=0;end
  else if(active&&de&&samples<256&&x==tx&&y==ty)begin
   working[{row,col}]<=skin;samples<=samples+1'b1;
   if(col==15)begin col<=0;row<=row+1'b1;end else col<=col+1'b1;
  end
 end
endmodule

来源：D:\yilingsi_fpga\Efinity_Project\技术文档\最新项目\10_13_ding_Ti60_Vision8_Gesture_ROI_Integrated\rtl\gesture_templates.v

// Three user-recorded silhouettes. Upper fingers have double weight.
// Serial 16-bit comparisons, five one-cell translations, no model framebuffer.
module gesture_templates #(parameter MAX_DISTANCE=66,MIN_MARGIN=12,MIN_SEPARATION=28)(
 input clk,rst_n,enable,clear,input[2:0]learn,
 input sample_done,sample_valid,input[255:0]bitmap,input[19:0]ratio,
 output reg result_done,output reg[2:0]raw_class,output reg[2:0]trained,
 output reg capture_done,capture_ok,output reg[1:0]capture_error,
 output reg[1:0]capture_count,output reg[9:0]best_distance);
 reg[255:0]model1,model2,model3,first,second,query;
 reg[7:0]aspect1,aspect2,aspect3,aspect_q;
 reg[2:0]learning;reg[2:0]state,variant;reg[1:0]which;reg[3:0]row;
 reg[9:0]sum,minimum,d1,d2,d3,previous_distance;
 reg[8:0]ones;
 wire[7:0]aspect=ratio>8159?8'd255:ratio[12:5];
 reg[15:0]qr,mr,pr;reg[255:0]selected;reg[7:0]selected_aspect,delta;
 reg[4:0]diff_count,prev_count,on_count;integer i;
 always @*begin
  selected=which==0?model1:(which==1?model2:model3);
  selected_aspect=which==0?aspect1:(which==1?aspect2:aspect3);
  delta=aspect_q>selected_aspect?aspect_q-selected_aspect:selected_aspect-aspect_q;
  qr=query[{row,4'b0}+:16];mr=selected[{row,4'b0}+:16];pr=first[{row,4'b0}+:16];
  case(variant)
   1:qr=qr<<1;2:qr=qr>>1;
   3:qr=row==0?16'd0:query[((row-1)*16)+:16];
   4:qr=row==15?16'd0:query[((row+1)*16)+:16];
  endcase
  diff_count=0;prev_count=0;on_count=0;
  for(i=0;i<16;i=i+1)begin
   diff_count=diff_count+(qr[i]^mr[i]);prev_count=prev_count+(qr[i]^pr[i]);on_count=on_count+qr[i];
  end
 end
 wire[9:0]add_distance=row<8?{4'd0,diff_count,1'b0}:{5'd0,diff_count};
 wire[9:0]finished=sum+add_distance;
 wire[9:0]penalty={3'd0,delta,1'b0};
 wire[9:0]final_distance=(finished<minimum?finished:minimum)+penalty;
 reg[9:0]best,next_best;reg[2:0]best_id;
 always @*begin
  best=1023;next_best=1023;best_id=0;
  if(trained[0])begin best=d1;best_id=1;end
  if(trained[1])begin if(d2<best)begin next_best=best;best=d2;best_id=2;end else next_best=d2;end
  if(trained[2])begin if(d3<best)begin next_best=best;best=d3;best_id=3;end else if(d3<next_best)next_best=d3;end
 end
 wire distinct=(learning==1||!trained[0]||d1>=MIN_SEPARATION)&&
  (learning==2||!trained[1]||d2>=MIN_SEPARATION)&&
  (learning==3||!trained[2]||d3>=MIN_SEPARATION);
 wire reasonable=ones>=40&&ones<=235&&aspect_q>=12&&aspect_q<=110;
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  result_done<=0;capture_done<=0;
  if(!rst_n||clear)begin
   model1<=0;model2<=0;model3<=0;aspect1<=0;aspect2<=0;aspect3<=0;trained<=0;
   first<=0;second<=0;query<=0;aspect_q<=0;learning<=0;capture_count<=0;capture_ok<=0;capture_error<=0;
   state<=0;variant<=0;which<=0;row<=0;sum<=0;minimum<=1023;d1<=1023;d2<=1023;d3<=1023;
   previous_distance<=0;ones<=0;raw_class<=0;best_distance<=1023;
  end else if(!enable)begin state<=0;learning<=0;capture_count<=0;raw_class<=0;end
  else if(learn>=1&&learn<=3)begin learning<=learn;capture_count<=0;capture_ok<=0;capture_error<=0;state<=0;raw_class<=0;end
  else case(state)
   0:if(sample_done)begin
    raw_class<=0;
    if(sample_valid)begin query<=bitmap;aspect_q<=aspect;state<=1;variant<=0;which<=0;row<=0;sum<=0;minimum<=1023;previous_distance<=0;ones<=0;end
    else begin result_done<=1;if(learning!=0)capture_count<=0;end
   end
   1:begin
    if(which==0&&variant==0)begin
     previous_distance<=previous_distance+(row<8?{4'd0,prev_count,1'b0}:{5'd0,prev_count});
     ones<=ones+on_count;
    end
    if(row!=15)begin row<=row+1'b1;sum<=finished;end
    else begin
     row<=0;sum<=0;
     if(variant!=4)begin variant<=variant+1'b1;if(finished<minimum)minimum<=finished;end
     else begin
      case(which)0:d1<=final_distance;1:d2<=final_distance;2:d3<=final_distance;endcase
      variant<=0;minimum<=1023;
      if(which==2)state<=2;else which<=which+1'b1;
     end
    end
   end
   2:begin
    state<=0;result_done<=1;best_distance<=best;
    if(learning!=0)begin
     if(!reasonable)begin capture_count<=0;capture_error<=1;end
     else if(capture_count==0||previous_distance>32)begin first<=query;capture_count<=1;capture_error<=0;end
     else if(capture_count==1)begin second<=query;capture_count<=2;end
     else begin
      capture_done<=1;capture_ok<=distinct;capture_error<=distinct?0:2;learning<=0;capture_count<=0;
      if(distinct)begin
       case(learning)
        1:begin model1<=(first&second)|(first&query)|(second&query);aspect1<=aspect_q;trained[0]<=1;end
        2:begin model2<=(first&second)|(first&query)|(second&query);aspect2<=aspect_q;trained[1]<=1;end
        3:begin model3<=(first&second)|(first&query)|(second&query);aspect3<=aspect_q;trained[2]<=1;end
       endcase
      end
     end
    end else if(trained==7&&reasonable&&best<=MAX_DISTANCE&&next_best>=best+MIN_MARGIN)raw_class<=best_id;
   end
   default:state<=0;
  endcase
 end
endmodule

// Independent open-palm evidence from the existing 16x16 clean silhouette.
// Multiple separated fingers in >=2 rows/columns plus a connected palm region.
// Serial processing and an iterative small divider; no new image RAM.
module gesture_palm_evidence(
 input clk,rst_n,enable,sample_done,sample_valid,input[255:0]bitmap,
 output reg done,valid,palm,output reg[7:0]lean);
 reg[255:0]q;reg[2:0]state;reg[3:0]row;
 reg[4:0]row_fingers,col_fingers,solid_rows,solid_cols;
 reg[8:0]total;reg[7:0]top_count;reg[10:0]top_sum;
 reg[15:0]line_r,line_c;reg[4:0]runs_r,runs_c,ones_r,ones_c,length_r,length_c;
 reg[7:0]moment;integer i;
 always @*begin
  line_r=q[{row,4'd0}+:16];line_c=0;
  for(i=0;i<16;i=i+1)line_c[i]=q[i*16+row];
  runs_r=0;runs_c=0;ones_r=0;ones_c=0;length_r=0;length_c=0;moment=0;
  for(i=0;i<16;i=i+1)begin
   if(line_r[i])begin length_r=length_r+1'b1;ones_r=ones_r+1'b1;moment=moment+i;end
   else begin if(length_r>=2)runs_r=runs_r+1'b1;length_r=0;end
   if(line_c[i])begin length_c=length_c+1'b1;ones_c=ones_c+1'b1;end
   else begin if(length_c>=2)runs_c=runs_c+1'b1;length_c=0;end
  end
  if(length_r>=2)runs_r=runs_r+1'b1;
  if(length_c>=2)runs_c=runs_c+1'b1;
 end
 reg[14:0]quotient;reg[8:0]remainder;reg[3:0]div_step;
 wire[9:0]trial={remainder,quotient[14]};
 wire take=trial>={2'd0,top_count};
 wire[14:0]next_q={quotient[13:0],take};
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  done<=0;
  if(!rst_n||!enable)begin
   q<=0;state<=0;row<=0;row_fingers<=0;col_fingers<=0;solid_rows<=0;solid_cols<=0;
   total<=0;top_count<=0;top_sum<=0;done<=0;valid<=0;palm<=0;lean<=120;quotient<=0;remainder<=0;div_step<=0;
  end else case(state)
   0:if(sample_done)begin
    if(sample_valid)begin
     q<=bitmap;state<=1;row<=0;row_fingers<=0;col_fingers<=0;solid_rows<=0;solid_cols<=0;total<=0;top_count<=0;top_sum<=0;
    end else begin valid<=0;palm<=0;done<=1;end
   end
   1:begin
    if(row<10&&runs_r>=3&&runs_r<=5&&ones_r>=6&&ones_r<=13)row_fingers<=row_fingers+1'b1;
    if(runs_c>=3&&runs_c<=5&&ones_c>=6&&ones_c<=13)col_fingers<=col_fingers+1'b1;
    if(row>=6&&runs_r==1&&ones_r>=8)solid_rows<=solid_rows+1'b1;
    if(runs_c==1&&ones_c>=8)solid_cols<=solid_cols+1'b1;
    total<=total+ones_r;
    if(row<8)begin top_count<=top_count+ones_r;top_sum<=top_sum+moment;end
    if(row==15)state<=2;else row<=row+1'b1;
   end
   2:begin
    valid<=top_count>=8&&total>=40&&total<=235;
    palm<=total>=60&&total<=225&&top_count>=12&&
      ((row_fingers>=2&&solid_rows>=3)||(col_fingers>=2&&solid_cols>=3));
    if(top_count==0)begin lean<=120;done<=1;state<=0;end
    else begin quotient<={top_sum,4'd0};remainder<=0;div_step<=0;state<=3;end
   end
   3:begin
    quotient<=next_q;remainder<=take?trial-top_count:trial;
    if(div_step==14)begin lean<=next_q[7:0];done<=1;state<=0;end else div_step<=div_step+1'b1;
   end
   default:state<=0;
  endcase
 end
endmodule

// Confirm the same target with a 5-of-7 vote. Hold at most two missing frames.
// Wave requires a confirmed open palm and two large direction reversals.
module gesture_stabilizer #(parameter WIDTH=1280,HEIGHT=720,
 WAVE_STEP=WIDTH/32,WAVE_WINDOW=90,WAVE_HOLD=30)(
 input clk,rst_n,enable,frame_done,fault,raw_valid,input[41:0]raw_box,
 input[2:0]raw_class,output reg box_valid,output reg[41:0]box,
 output reg[2:0]gesture);
 reg[2:0]history[0:5];reg[1:0]missing;
 wire[11:0]cx_sum={1'b0,raw_box[10:0]}+{1'b0,raw_box[21:11]};
 wire[11:0]px_sum={1'b0,box[10:0]}+{1'b0,box[21:11]};
 wire[10:0]cy_sum={1'b0,raw_box[31:22]}+{1'b0,raw_box[41:32]};
 wire[10:0]py_sum={1'b0,box[31:22]}+{1'b0,box[41:32]};
 wire[10:0]cx=cx_sum[11:1],px=px_sum[11:1];
 wire[9:0]cy=cy_sum[10:1],py=py_sum[10:1];
 wire[10:0]dx=cx>px?cx-px:px-cx;
 wire[9:0]dy=cy>py?cy-py:py-cy;
 wire[10:0]pw=box[21:11]-box[10:0]+1,rw=raw_box[21:11]-raw_box[10:0]+1;
 wire[9:0]ph=box[41:32]-box[31:22]+1,rh=raw_box[41:32]-raw_box[31:22]+1;
 wire same=box_valid&&dx<=(pw>>1)+WIDTH/16&&dy<=(ph>>1)+HEIGHT/16&&
           {1'b0,rw}*2>=pw&&{1'b0,pw}*2>=rw&&{1'b0,rh}*2>=ph&&{1'b0,ph}*2>=rh;
 reg[2:0]winner;integer i,k,n;
 always @*begin
  winner=0;n=0;
  for(k=1;k<=3;k=k+1)begin
   n=(raw_valid&&raw_class==k)?1:0;
   for(i=0;i<6;i=i+1)if(history[i]==k)n=n+1;
   if(n>=5)winner=k;
  end
 end
 reg[1:0]direction,turns;reg[10:0]extreme;
 reg[6:0]age;reg[5:0]wave_hold;
 integer j;
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  if(!rst_n||!enable||fault)begin
   box_valid<=0;box<=0;gesture<=0;missing<=0;
   direction<=0;turns<=0;extreme<=0;age<=0;wave_hold<=0;
   for(j=0;j<6;j=j+1)history[j]<=0;
  end else if(frame_done)begin
   if(!raw_valid)begin
    for(j=5;j>0;j=j-1)history[j]<=history[j-1];history[0]<=0;
    if(age<WAVE_WINDOW)age<=age+1'b1;
    if(missing==2)begin box_valid<=0;box<=0;gesture<=0;direction<=0;turns<=0;age<=0;wave_hold<=0;end
    else begin missing<=missing+1'b1;if(gesture==1)gesture<=0;end
   end else if(!same)begin
    box_valid<=1;box<=raw_box;gesture<=0;missing<=0;
    for(j=0;j<6;j=j+1)history[j]<=0;history[0]<=raw_class;
    direction<=0;turns<=0;extreme<=cx;age<=0;wave_hold<=0;
   end else begin
    box_valid<=1;box<=raw_box;missing<=0;
    for(j=5;j>0;j=j-1)history[j]<=history[j-1];history[0]<=raw_class;
    gesture<=winner==1?3'd0:winner;
    if(winner!=1)begin direction<=0;turns<=0;age<=0;wave_hold<=0;extreme<=cx;end
    else if(wave_hold!=0)begin gesture<=1;wave_hold<=wave_hold-1'b1;end
    else if(raw_class!=1)begin if(age<WAVE_WINDOW)age<=age+1'b1;end
    else begin
     if(age>=WAVE_WINDOW)begin direction<=0;turns<=0;age<=0;extreme<=cx;end
     else begin
      age<=age+1'b1;
      if(direction==0)begin
       if(cx>extreme&&cx-extreme>=WAVE_STEP)begin direction<=1;extreme<=cx;end
       else if(cx<extreme&&extreme-cx>=WAVE_STEP)begin direction<=2;extreme<=cx;end
      end else if(direction==1)begin
       if(cx>extreme)extreme<=cx;
       else if(extreme-cx>=WAVE_STEP)begin
        direction<=2;extreme<=cx;
        if(turns==1)begin gesture<=1;wave_hold<=WAVE_HOLD;turns<=0;direction<=0;age<=0;end
        else turns<=turns+1'b1;
       end
      end else begin
       if(cx<extreme)extreme<=cx;
       else if(cx-extreme>=WAVE_STEP)begin
        direction<=1;extreme<=cx;
        if(turns==1)begin gesture<=1;wave_hold<=WAVE_HOLD;turns<=0;direction<=0;age<=0;end
        else turns<=turns+1'b1;
       end
      end
     end
    end
   end
  end
 end
endmodule

// Palm evidence and motion are separate: tolerate up to six uncertain frames.
// A wave is an excursion and return, either hand translation or wrist rocking.
// Static palm, slow drift, vertical-only motion and non-palms never trigger.
module gesture_wave #(parameter WIDTH=1280,HEIGHT=720,WINDOW=180,HOLD_FRAMES=60,
 LEAN_STEP=18,GRACE=6)(
 input clk,rst_n,enable,frame_done,fault,raw_valid,input[41:0]raw_box,
 input[2:0]raw_class,input lean_valid,input[7:0]lean,
 output reg active,output reg[2:0]phase);
 reg tracked;reg[41:0]previous_box;reg[3:0]history;reg[2:0]missing;
 reg[3:0]credit;reg[7:0]age,hold_count;reg[1:0]dir_x,dir_l;
 reg[10:0]extreme_x,min_x,max_x;reg[7:0]extreme_l,min_l,max_l;
 wire[11:0]cx2={1'b0,raw_box[10:0]}+raw_box[21:11];
 wire[11:0]px2={1'b0,previous_box[10:0]}+previous_box[21:11];
 wire[10:0]cy2={1'b0,raw_box[31:22]}+raw_box[41:32];
 wire[10:0]py2={1'b0,previous_box[31:22]}+previous_box[41:32];
 wire[10:0]cx=cx2[11:1],px=px2[11:1];
 wire[9:0]cy=cy2[10:1],py=py2[10:1];
 wire[10:0]w=raw_box[21:11]-raw_box[10:0]+1,pw=previous_box[21:11]-previous_box[10:0]+1;
 wire[9:0]h=raw_box[41:32]-raw_box[31:22]+1,ph=previous_box[41:32]-previous_box[31:22]+1;
 wire[10:0]dx=cx>px?cx-px:px-cx;wire[9:0]dy=cy>py?cy-py:py-cy;
 wire same=tracked&&dx<=pw/2+WIDTH/16&&dy<=ph/2+HEIGHT/16&&w*2>=pw&&pw*2>=w&&h*2>=ph&&ph*2>=h;
 wire[10:0]step_x=(w>>3)<WIDTH/64?WIDTH/64:((w>>3)>WIDTH/24?WIDTH/24:(w>>3));
 wire[2:0]votes={2'd0,history[0]}+{2'd0,history[1]}+{2'd0,history[2]}+{2'd0,history[3]}+(raw_valid&&raw_class==1);
 wire confirmed=votes>=3;
 wire armed=credit!=0||confirmed;
 wire x_back=(dir_x==1&&cx<extreme_x&&extreme_x-cx>=step_x)||
             (dir_x==2&&cx>extreme_x&&cx-extreme_x>=step_x);
 wire l_back=lean_valid&&((dir_l==1&&lean<extreme_l&&extreme_l-lean>=LEAN_STEP)||
                         (dir_l==2&&lean>extreme_l&&lean-extreme_l>=LEAN_STEP));
 always @(posedge clk)begin
  if(!rst_n||!enable||fault)begin
   active<=0;phase<=0;tracked<=0;previous_box<=0;history<=0;missing<=0;credit<=0;age<=0;hold_count<=0;
   dir_x<=0;dir_l<=0;extreme_x<=0;extreme_l<=120;min_x<=0;max_x<=0;min_l<=120;max_l<=120;
  end else if(frame_done)begin
   history<={history[2:0],raw_valid&&raw_class==1};
   if(!raw_valid)begin
    active<=0;phase<=0;
    if(credit!=0)credit<=credit-1'b1;
    if(missing<3)missing<=missing+1'b1;
    if(missing>=2)begin tracked<=0;credit<=0;age<=0;hold_count<=0;dir_x<=0;dir_l<=0;history<=0;end
   end else if(!same)begin
    tracked<=1;previous_box<=raw_box;history<={3'd0,raw_class==1};missing<=0;credit<=0;age<=0;hold_count<=0;
    dir_x<=0;dir_l<=0;extreme_x<=cx;extreme_l<=lean;min_x<=cx;max_x<=cx;min_l<=lean;max_l<=lean;active<=0;phase<=1;
   end else begin
    previous_box<=raw_box;missing<=0;active<=0;
    if(raw_class==2||raw_class==3)begin
     credit<=0;history<=0;age<=0;hold_count<=0;dir_x<=0;dir_l<=0;extreme_x<=cx;extreme_l<=lean;min_x<=cx;max_x<=cx;min_l<=lean;max_l<=lean;phase<=1;
    end else begin
     if(raw_class==1&&(confirmed||credit!=0))credit<=GRACE;
     else if(credit!=0)credit<=credit-1'b1;
     if(!armed)begin
      phase<=1;age<=0;hold_count<=0;dir_x<=0;dir_l<=0;extreme_x<=cx;extreme_l<=lean;min_x<=cx;max_x<=cx;min_l<=lean;max_l<=lean;
     end else if(hold_count!=0)begin active<=1;phase<=4;hold_count<=hold_count-1'b1;end
     else if(age>=WINDOW)begin phase<=2;age<=0;dir_x<=0;dir_l<=0;extreme_x<=cx;extreme_l<=lean;min_x<=cx;max_x<=cx;min_l<=lean;max_l<=lean;end
     else begin
      age<=age+1'b1;phase<=(dir_x!=0||dir_l!=0)?3:2;
      // Do not generate an event using a stale lean value from a blurred frame.
      if((x_back||l_back)&&raw_class==1)begin active<=1;phase<=4;hold_count<=HOLD_FRAMES-1;age<=0;dir_x<=0;dir_l<=0;min_x<=cx;max_x<=cx;min_l<=lean;max_l<=lean;end
      else begin
       if(dir_x==0)begin
        // Track both ends before choosing direction. A hand starting in the
        // middle must not require twice the useful peak-to-peak amplitude.
        if(cx<min_x)min_x<=cx;if(cx>max_x)max_x<=cx;
        if(cx>min_x&&cx-min_x>=step_x)begin dir_x<=1;extreme_x<=cx;end
        else if(cx<max_x&&max_x-cx>=step_x)begin dir_x<=2;extreme_x<=cx;end
       end else if(dir_x==1&&cx>extreme_x)extreme_x<=cx;
       else if(dir_x==2&&cx<extreme_x)extreme_x<=cx;
       if(lean_valid)begin
        if(dir_l==0)begin
         if(lean<min_l)min_l<=lean;if(lean>max_l)max_l<=lean;
         if(lean>min_l&&lean-min_l>=LEAN_STEP)begin dir_l<=1;extreme_l<=lean;end
         else if(lean<max_l&&max_l-lean>=LEAN_STEP)begin dir_l<=2;extreme_l<=lean;end
        end else if(dir_l==1&&lean>extreme_l)extreme_l<=lean;
        else if(dir_l==2&&lean<extreme_l)extreme_l<=lean;
       end
      end
     end
    end
   end
  end
 end
endmodule

rtl/gesture_wizard.v
来源：D:\yilingsi_fpga\Efinity_Project\技术文档\最新项目\10_13_ding_Ti60_Vision8_Gesture_ROI_Integrated\rtl\gesture_wizard.v
